tot_music_competition_rebuild_idol_candidates_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_country
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_idol_candidates }
		clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_idol_candidates
	}
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_candidate_count value = 0 }
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			is_character_alive = yes
			is_historical = yes
			OR = {
				has_trait = tot_group_sheet_023_trait
				has_trait = tot_group_sheet_060_trait
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_candidate
		scope:tot_music_competition_country = {
			add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_candidates target = scope:tot_music_competition_candidate }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_candidate_count add = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# Registration lasts twelve months. Once at least eight teams have registered,
# the field is locked for a further six-month preparation period before play.
tot_music_competition_start_if_ready_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open }
		# Rebuild only at the registration boundary. The public list is otherwise
		# updated by registration actions, but that cached count can be stale when
		# a save is loaded after the AI's hidden registration pass.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining
				var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining <= 0
			}
			tot_music_competition_build_field_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining
				var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining <= 0
				has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants
				var:tot_music_competition_participants >= 8
			}
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_deadline
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum
			# A new season must never inherit the previous final's stop flag.
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_completed
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_award_announced
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_weekly_tick_scheduled
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_next_tick
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_closed value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_open value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_months value = 0 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline months = 6 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining value = 182 }
			tot_music_competition_schedule_weekly_tick_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining
				var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining <= 0
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants }
					var:tot_music_competition_participants < 8
				}
			}
			# The annual date has passed, but this is still pre-competition. Keep
			# registration open only until the eight-team minimum is reached.
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum value = 1 }
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_insufficient_participants
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining value = 0 }
		}
	}
}

tot_music_competition_begin_after_preparation_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open }
		tot_music_competition_build_field_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining
				var:tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining <= 0
				has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants
				var:tot_music_competition_participants >= 8
			}
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_closed
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_months
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_completed
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_weekly_tick_scheduled
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_next_tick
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_stage value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_round value = 0 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_score value = 0 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualifying_deadline weeks = 26 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining value = 182 }
			tot_music_competition_schedule_weekly_tick_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# The active player owns the season state. This keeps the scheduled events,
# GUI state, and save-game migration in one scope.
tot_music_competition_ensure_registration_cycle_effect = {
	# One-time migration from the short-lived Vienna-host build. Its scheduled
	# event may be queued on a different country, so discard only that stale
	# queue and let this player-owned state schedule a fresh weekly heartbeat.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_player_state_migrated } }
		every_country = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_global_host }
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_weekly_tick_scheduled
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_next_tick
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_player_state_migrated value = 1 }
	}
	# Recover saves made while a prior version could set the closed flag without
	# creating its preparation timer.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_closed
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage }
		}
		tot_music_competition_build_field_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants
				var:tot_music_competition_participants >= 8
			}
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_open value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_months value = 0 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline months = 6 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining value = 182 }
		}
		else = {
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_closed
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_open value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining value = 365 }
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_insufficient_participants
			tot_music_competition_build_field_effect = yes
			tot_music_competition_schedule_ai_registration_effect = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_player = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_closed }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_completed }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_open value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_months value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_deadline months = 12 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline months = 18 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining value = 365 }
		tot_music_competition_build_field_effect = yes
		tot_music_competition_schedule_ai_registration_effect = yes
		tot_music_competition_schedule_weekly_tick_effect = yes
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_track value = 1 } }
	}
	# Existing saves can already be in an open phase but lack a valid queued
	# heartbeat. The guard inside this effect prevents duplicate weekly events.
	tot_music_competition_schedule_weekly_tick_effect = yes
}

# Every active competition owns exactly one pending hidden weekly event. The
# timer variables retain the calendar deadlines for the GUI while the event
# resolves scoring and phase transitions without player input.
tot_music_competition_schedule_weekly_tick_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
				OR = {
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open
				}
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_completed }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_weekly_tick_scheduled }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_weekly_tick_scheduled value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_next_tick days = 7 }
		trigger_event = { id = tot_music_competition.1 days = 7 }
	}
}

# AI has no GUI click path. It receives one hidden registration pass per season
# instead of adding preset teams during every country's monthly pulse.
tot_music_competition_schedule_ai_registration_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_ai_registration_pending }
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants }
				AND = {
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants
					var:tot_music_competition_participants < 8
				}
			}
		}
		# The initial pass can leave fewer than eight entries. Keep retrying while
		# the late-registration window is open instead of leaving the season stuck.
		# Use a one-day delay: a new game begins with an empty roster, but the
		# public field is populated promptly once the simulation has started.
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_ai_registration_pending value = 1 }
		trigger_event = { id = tot_music_competition.2 days = 1 }
	}
}

tot_music_competition_run_ai_registration_effect = {
	every_country = {
		tot_music_competition_ai_band_registration = yes
	}
	tot_music_competition_build_field_effect = yes
}

tot_music_competition_progress_week_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
			OR = {
				AND = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining > 0 }
				has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining > 0 }
			if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining > 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining add = -7 } }
			else = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining value = 0 } }
		}
		# AI registrations are delayed through a single hidden event.
		tot_music_competition_schedule_ai_registration_effect = yes
		tot_music_competition_build_field_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining var:tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining > 0 }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining > 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining add = -7 } }
		else = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining value = 0 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage var:tot_music_competition_stage = 1 has_variable = tot_music_competition_round var:tot_music_competition_round < 25 }
		change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_round add = 1 }
		change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_score add = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining var:tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining > 0 }
			if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining > 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining add = -7 } }
			else = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining value = 0 } }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 } tot_music_competition_add_weekly_scores_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track OR = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 2 var:tot_music_competition_track = 3 } } tot_music_competition_add_weekly_idol_scores_effect = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage var:tot_music_competition_stage = 1 has_variable = tot_music_competition_round var:tot_music_competition_round >= 25 }
		change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_score add = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 } tot_music_competition_add_weekly_scores_effect = yes tot_music_competition_prepare_band_qualifying_scores_effect = yes tot_music_competition_build_qualified_band_field_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track OR = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 2 var:tot_music_competition_track = 3 } } tot_music_competition_add_weekly_idol_scores_effect = yes tot_music_competition_prepare_idol_qualifying_scores_effect = yes tot_music_competition_build_qualified_idol_field_effect = yes }
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_qualifying_deadline
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_stage value = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_round value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualified_slots value = 8 }
		tot_music_competition_clear_final_bracket_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_deadline weeks = 26 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining value = 182 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage var:tot_music_competition_stage = 2 has_variable = tot_music_competition_round var:tot_music_competition_round < 26 }
		change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_round add = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining var:tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining > 0 }
			if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining > 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining add = -7 } }
			else = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining value = 0 } }
		}
		# Resolve each bracket stage only after its final week has elapsed.
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_effect = yes
		if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_round >= 26 } tot_music_competition_finish_final_effect = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		# Recovery for saves made after the final week but before the scheduled event
		# could fire. This must complete immediately instead of scheduling week 27.
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage var:tot_music_competition_stage = 2 has_variable = tot_music_competition_round var:tot_music_competition_round >= 26 }
		tot_music_competition_finish_final_effect = yes
	}
	# Calendar-state transitions use the same day counters shown in the GUI.
	# This also repairs saves where a previous version reached day zero before
	# the monthly maintenance pass could see the phase boundary.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining <= 0 } tot_music_competition_start_if_ready_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining var:tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining <= 0 } tot_music_competition_begin_after_preparation_effect = yes }
}

# The score variables live on each participating country. The active player's
# panel therefore only needs a read-only phase snapshot plus rebuilt lists.
tot_music_competition_sync_spectator_view_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_live_host
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_player = yes NOT = { this = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host } }
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_observer
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_spectator_view value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_track value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_track } }
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open } }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_public_registration_open value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_deadline value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_registration_deadline }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline }
		}
		else = {
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_public_registration_open
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_deadline
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline
		}
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_closed } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_closed value = 1 } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_closed }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum value = 1 } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_participants value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_participants } }
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open } }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_open value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline }
		}
		else = {
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining
			remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline
		}
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_stage value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_stage } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_round value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_round } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_stage remove_variable = tot_music_competition_round }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_completed } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_completed value = 1 } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_completed }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_completed } }
			# The observer rebuilds its own read-only lists from every country's
			# registered teams. No host-scoped list is copied through a nested scope.
			tot_music_competition_build_band_team_field_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage var:tot_music_competition_stage = 2 has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 }
			tot_music_competition_build_qualified_band_field_effect = yes
			tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# A registration window belongs to the host only. A prior GUI bug could create
# a second local window during preparation; remove only those stray entries.
tot_music_competition_clear_unauthorized_registration_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_live_host
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { this = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host }
			OR = {
				scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open } }
				AND = {
					scope:tot_music_competition_live_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining <= 0 }
				}
			}
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
		}
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_deadline
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_months
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_score
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_score
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_score
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_score
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_score
	}
}

# Called by a country's activity page after a tag switch. It mirrors only the
# current global phase and rebuilds the display from all countries' real teams.
tot_music_competition_refresh_spectator_view_effect = {
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_global_host }
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_live_host
	}
	# Remove the old local registration window immediately after a tag switch.
	# Legitimate participants only store their team flags, never this host-only flag.
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_global_host }
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
			scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open } }
		}
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_deadline
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_months
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_score
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_score
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_score
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_score
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_score
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_global_host }
			scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= {
				OR = {
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage
				}
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_observer
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_spectator_view value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_track value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_track }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_open } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_public_registration_open value = 1 } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_deadline value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_registration_deadline } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_public_registration_open remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_days_remaining remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_deadline remove_variable = tot_music_competition_season_start_deadline }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_closed } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_closed value = 1 } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_closed }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum value = 1 } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_registration_waiting_for_minimum }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_participants value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_participants } }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_open value = 1 } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_open remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_days_remaining remove_variable = tot_music_competition_preparation_deadline }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_stage value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_stage } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_round value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_round } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_stage remove_variable = tot_music_competition_round }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_qualifying_days_remaining }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining value = scope:tot_music_competition_live_host.var:tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining }
		if = { limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_live_host ?= { has_variable = tot_music_competition_completed } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_completed value = 1 } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_completed }
		if = {
			limit = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_completed } }
			tot_music_competition_build_band_team_field_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 var:tot_music_competition_stage = 2 }
			tot_music_competition_build_qualified_band_field_effect = yes
			tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# A dissolved band cannot remain in a public field or suppress the player alert.
tot_music_competition_clear_invalid_band_entries_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_created } } remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_score }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_created } } remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_score }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_3_created } } remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_score }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_4_created } } remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_score }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_5_created } } remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_score }

	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registered has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered has_variable = tot_band_1_created } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered has_variable = tot_band_2_created } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered has_variable = tot_band_3_created } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered has_variable = tot_band_4_created } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered has_variable = tot_band_5_created } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_band_registration_count > 0 } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registered value = 1 } }
		else = { remove_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registered }
	}
}

# The monthly pulse. A country can register up to three eligible bands.
# AI cannot click the player GUI, so it uses the same registration state during
# the monthly pulse.
tot_music_competition_ai_band_registration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_player = yes }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registered }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count value = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_player = yes }
			var:tot_music_competition_band_registration_count < 3
			has_variable = tot_band_1_created
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered }
		}
		random_list = {
		35 = {
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_score value = 0 }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 }
		}
		65 = { }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_player = yes }
			var:tot_music_competition_band_registration_count < 3
			has_variable = tot_band_2_created
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered }
		}
		random_list = {
		35 = {
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_score value = 0 }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 }
		}
		65 = { }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_player = yes }
			var:tot_music_competition_band_registration_count < 3
			has_variable = tot_band_3_created
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered }
		}
		random_list = {
		35 = {
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_3_score value = 0 }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 }
		}
		65 = { }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_player = yes }
			var:tot_music_competition_band_registration_count < 3
			has_variable = tot_band_4_created
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered }
		}
		random_list = {
		35 = {
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_4_score value = 0 }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 }
		}
		65 = { }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_player = yes }
			var:tot_music_competition_band_registration_count < 3
			has_variable = tot_band_5_created
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered }
		}
		random_list = {
		35 = {
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered value = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_5_score value = 0 }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count add = 1 }
		}
		65 = { }
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registration_count var:tot_music_competition_band_registration_count > 0 } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_registered value = 1 } }
}

tot_music_competition_add_band_1_team_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_team_country
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_participants value = 0 } }
	scope:tot_music_competition_host = {
		if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants < 64 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_field target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_field target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } tot_music_competition_assign_band_1_display_slot_effect = yes }
			if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 0 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_1 target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 1 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_1 target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 2 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_1 target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 3 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_1 target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } }

			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 12 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_1 target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 13 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_1 target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 14 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_1 target = scope:tot_music_competition_team_country } }

		if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants < 64 } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_participants add = 1 } }
	}
}

# Build a band bracket from registered teams. A country may occupy two team slots.
tot_music_competition_build_band_team_field_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_host
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_participants value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_field } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_field }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_1_field } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_1_field }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_2_field } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_2_field }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_3_field } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_3_field }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_4_field } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_4_field }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_5_field } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_band_5_field }
	tot_music_competition_clear_band_display_slots_effect = yes
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_1 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_2 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_3 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_4 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_5 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01_band_5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_1 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_2 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_3 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_4 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_5 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02_band_5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_1 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_2 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_3 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_4 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_5 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03_band_5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_1 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_2 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_3 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_4 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_5 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04_band_5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_1 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_2 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_3 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_4 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_5 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05_band_5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_1 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_2 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_3 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_4 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_5 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06_band_5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_1 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_2 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_3 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_4 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_5 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07_band_5 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_1 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_2 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_3 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_4 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_5 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08_band_5 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered has_variable = tot_band_1_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_1_team_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered has_variable = tot_band_2_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_2_team_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered has_variable = tot_band_3_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_3_team_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered has_variable = tot_band_4_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_4_team_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered has_variable = tot_band_5_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_5_team_effect = yes }
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { this = scope:tot_music_competition_host }
			scope:tot_music_competition_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants var:tot_music_competition_participants < 64 }
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registered
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_ai_country
		scope:tot_music_competition_host = {
			scope:tot_music_competition_ai_country = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered has_variable = tot_band_1_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_1_team_effect = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered has_variable = tot_band_2_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_2_team_effect = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered has_variable = tot_band_3_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_3_team_effect = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered has_variable = tot_band_4_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_4_team_effect = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered has_variable = tot_band_5_created } tot_music_competition_add_band_5_team_effect = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

tot_music_competition_build_field_effect = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_track value = 1 } }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 }
		tot_music_competition_build_band_team_field_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		tot_music_competition_build_legacy_field_effect = yes
	}
}

# Legacy participant builder retained for the individual-event prototype.

tot_music_competition_build_legacy_field_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_host
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_participants value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_field }
		clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_field
	}
	# The visible bracket uses eight fixed first-round pairings.  Keeping the
	# pair lists separate means the GUI always has a real two-country match.
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_01 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_02 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_03 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_04 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_05 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_06 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_07 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08 } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_match_08 }
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_player_entry
	add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_field target = scope:tot_music_competition_player_entry }
	add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_01 target = scope:tot_music_competition_player_entry }
	change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_participants add = 1 }

	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_player = yes }
				scope:tot_music_competition_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_participants var:tot_music_competition_participants < 64 }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					scope:tot_music_competition_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 }
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registered
					any_scope_character = {
						is_character_alive = yes
						is_historical = yes
						OR = {
							has_trait = tot_group_sheet_052_trait
							has_trait = tot_group_sheet_053_trait
							has_trait = tot_group_sheet_054_trait
							has_trait = tot_group_sheet_055_trait
							has_trait = tot_group_sheet_056_trait
							has_trait = tot_group_sheet_058_trait
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					scope:tot_music_competition_host = {
						has_variable = tot_music_competition_track
						OR = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 2 var:tot_music_competition_track = 3 }
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						is_character_alive = yes
						is_historical = yes
						OR = {
							has_trait = tot_group_sheet_023_trait
							has_trait = tot_group_sheet_060_trait
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_ai_entry
		scope:tot_music_competition_host = {
			add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_field target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry }
			if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 1 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_01 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 2 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_02 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 3 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_02 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 4 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_03 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 5 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_03 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 6 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_04 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 7 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_04 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 8 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_05 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 9 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_05 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 10 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_06 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 11 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_06 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 12 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_07 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 13 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_07 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 14 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_08 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:tot_music_competition_participants = 15 } add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_match_08 target = scope:tot_music_competition_ai_entry } }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_participants add = 1 }
		}
	}
}
# A round-level occurrence is shared by a country's entries, so it represents
# preparation, venue, or publicity conditions rather than a random character
# failure.  It stays deliberately small compared with growth multipliers.
tot_music_competition_roll_event_modifier_effect = {
	random_list = {
		50 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_event_modifier value = 0 } }
		20 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_event_modifier value = 0.1 } }
		20 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_event_modifier value = -0.1 } }
		5 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_event_modifier value = 0.15 } }
		5 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_event_modifier value = -0.15 } }
	}
}

# --- Idol qualifying score table --------------------------------------------
# Idol scores are kept on the participating country, but never share the
# band's score variables.  Solo and group events therefore remain comparable
# at the activity level without making one event overwrite another.
tot_music_competition_add_weekly_idol_scores_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_idol_host
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 2 }
					any_scope_character = {
						is_character_alive = yes
						is_historical = yes
						OR = { has_trait = tot_group_sheet_023_trait has_trait = tot_group_sheet_060_trait }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 3 }
					any_scope_character = {
						is_character_alive = yes
						is_historical = yes
						OR = { has_trait = tot_group_sheet_023_trait has_trait = tot_group_sheet_060_trait }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		tot_music_competition_rebuild_idol_candidates_effect = yes
		tot_music_competition_roll_event_modifier_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 2 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_score } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_score value = 0 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_raw_score } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_raw_score value = 0 } }
			random_list = {
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_performance value = 8 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_performance value = 9 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_performance value = 10 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_performance value = 11 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_performance value = 12 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_performance value = 13 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_performance value = 14 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_performance value = 15 } }
			}
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_multiplier value = { value = tot_music_competition_idol_solo_multiplier_value multiply = 100 } }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_raw_score add = { value = var:tot_music_competition_idol_performance multiply = tot_music_competition_idol_solo_multiplier_value } }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_score add = 1 }
			if = { limit = { tot_music_competition_idol_solo_multiplier_value >= 3 } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_score add = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 3 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_group_score } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_score value = 0 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_group_raw_score } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_raw_score value = 0 } }
			random_list = {
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance value = 8 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance value = 9 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance value = 10 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance value = 11 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance value = 12 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance value = 13 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance value = 14 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance value = 15 } }
			}
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_multiplier value = { value = tot_music_competition_idol_group_multiplier_value multiply = 100 } }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_raw_score add = { value = var:tot_music_competition_idol_group_performance multiply = tot_music_competition_idol_group_multiplier_value } }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_score add = 1 }
			if = { limit = { tot_music_competition_idol_group_multiplier_value >= 3 } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_score add = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

# Convert raw accumulated performance into the old 0-54 ranking scale only at
# the end of qualifying.  The displayed raw score remains untouched.
tot_music_competition_prepare_idol_qualifying_scores_effect = {
	every_country = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_raw_score }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_score value = tot_music_competition_idol_solo_rank_score_value }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_group_raw_score }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_group_score value = tot_music_competition_idol_group_rank_score_value }
		}
	}
}

tot_music_competition_prepare_band_qualifying_scores_effect = {
	every_country = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_raw_score }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_score value = tot_music_competition_band_1_rank_score_value }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_raw_score }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_score value = tot_music_competition_band_2_rank_score_value }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_raw_score }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_3_score value = tot_music_competition_band_3_rank_score_value }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_raw_score }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_4_score value = tot_music_competition_band_4_rank_score_value }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_raw_score }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_5_score value = tot_music_competition_band_5_rank_score_value }
		}
	}
}

tot_music_competition_build_qualified_idol_field_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_idol_host
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualified_count value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_qualified_idol_field } clear_variable_list = tot_music_competition_qualified_idol_field }
	every_country = {
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_qualified } remove_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_qualified }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_qualified }
			scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = { var:tot_music_competition_qualified_count < 8 }
			OR = {
				AND = { scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 2 } has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_score var:tot_music_competition_idol_score >= 54 }
				AND = { scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 3 } has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_group_score var:tot_music_competition_idol_group_score >= 54 }
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_idol_candidate
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_qualified value = 1 }
		scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = {
			add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_qualified_idol_field target = scope:tot_music_competition_idol_candidate }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualified_count add = 1 }
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_qualified }

				AND = { scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = { var:tot_music_competition_track = 2 } has_variable = tot_music_competition_idol_score var:tot_music_competition_idol_score >= 53 }

			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_idol_candidate
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_qualified value = 1 }
		scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = {

			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_idol_candidate
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_idol_qualified value = 1 }
		scope:tot_music_competition_idol_host = {
			add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_music_competition_qualified_idol_field target = scope:tot_music_competition_idol_candidate }

# --- Qualifying score table -------------------------------------------------
# Add one weekly score roll to every registered team. Scores are kept on the
# country with one counter per band, so two teams from the same country remain
# separate competitors. The old 1/2 point roll is retained as a rank score for
# compatibility with the existing qualifying table. Raw performance is stored
# separately and is the value used by the new activity rules.
tot_music_competition_refresh_band_mastery_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_mastery_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_mastery_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_3_mastery_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_4_mastery_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_5_mastery_bonus value = 0 }
	save_scope_as = tot_music_competition_score_country
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { is_character_alive = yes has_variable = tot_band_id }
		if = {
			limit = { var:tot_band_id = 1 }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_guitarist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_guitarist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_bassist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_bassist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_keyboardist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_keyboardist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_dj_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_dj_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_drummer_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_drummer_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_vocalist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_vocalist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:tot_band_id = 2 }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_guitarist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_guitarist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_bassist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_bassist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_keyboardist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_keyboardist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_dj_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_dj_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_drummer_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_drummer_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_vocalist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_2_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_vocalist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:tot_band_id = 3 }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_guitarist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_3_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_guitarist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_bassist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_3_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_bassist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_keyboardist_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_3_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_keyboardist_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_dj_mastery } scope:tot_music_competition_score_country = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_3_mastery_bonus add = { value = var:tot_character_growth_dj_mastery multiply = 0.2 } } } }

tot_music_competition_add_weekly_scores_effect = {
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_registered }
		tot_music_competition_refresh_band_mastery_effect = yes
		tot_music_competition_roll_event_modifier_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_score } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_score value = 0 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_raw_score } } set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_raw_score value = 0 } }
			random_list = {
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_performance value = 8 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_performance value = 9 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_performance value = 10 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_performance value = 11 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_performance value = 12 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_performance value = 13 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_performance value = 14 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_performance value = 15 } }
			}
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_multiplier value = { value = tot_music_competition_band_1_multiplier_value multiply = 100 } }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_raw_score add = { value = var:tot_music_competition_band_1_performance multiply = tot_music_competition_band_1_multiplier_value } }
			change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_score add = 1 }
			if = { limit = { tot_music_competition_band_1_multiplier_value >= 3 } change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_score add = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered }

# The qualifying phase is a score table. Highest scores are processed first;
# ties use the game's deterministic country iteration order.
tot_music_competition_build_qualified_band_field_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_host
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_qualified_count value = 0 }
