			}
			scope:tot_music_competition_candidate = { tot_music_competition_add_qualified_band_2_effect = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:tot_music_competition_host = { var:tot_music_competition_qualified_count < 8 }
				scope:tot_music_competition_candidate = {
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_score
					var:tot_music_competition_band_3_score = 0
				}
			}
			scope:tot_music_competition_candidate = { tot_music_competition_add_qualified_band_3_effect = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:tot_music_competition_host = { var:tot_music_competition_qualified_count < 8 }
				scope:tot_music_competition_candidate = {
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_score
					var:tot_music_competition_band_4_score = 0
				}
			}
			scope:tot_music_competition_candidate = { tot_music_competition_add_qualified_band_4_effect = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:tot_music_competition_host = { var:tot_music_competition_qualified_count < 8 }
				scope:tot_music_competition_candidate = {
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered
					has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_score
					var:tot_music_competition_band_5_score = 0
				}
			}
			scope:tot_music_competition_candidate = { tot_music_competition_add_qualified_band_5_effect = yes }
		}
	}
	remove_variable = tot_music_competition_qualified_count
}

# The eight finalists are already ordered by their qualifying score.  The
# knockout prototype advances the higher seed in each pairing, while keeping
# every round in a separate list for the activity GUI.
tot_music_competition_clear_final_slot_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_1 } clear_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_2 } clear_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_3 } clear_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_4 } clear_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_5 } clear_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_5 }
}

tot_music_competition_clear_final_bracket_effect = {
	tot_music_competition_clear_final_slot_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_four_01 }
	tot_music_competition_clear_final_slot_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_four_02 }
	tot_music_competition_clear_final_slot_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_four_03 }
	tot_music_competition_clear_final_slot_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_four_04 }
	tot_music_competition_clear_final_slot_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_two_01 }
	tot_music_competition_clear_final_slot_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_two_02 }
	tot_music_competition_clear_final_slot_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_champion }
	remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_quarterfinal_resolved
	remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_semifinal_resolved
	remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_champion_resolved
}

tot_music_competition_advance_band_final_slot_effect = {
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SOURCE$_band_1 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { add_to_variable_list = { name = $TARGET$_band_1 target = prev } } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SOURCE$_band_2 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { add_to_variable_list = { name = $TARGET$_band_2 target = prev } } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SOURCE$_band_3 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { add_to_variable_list = { name = $TARGET$_band_3 target = prev } } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SOURCE$_band_4 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { add_to_variable_list = { name = $TARGET$_band_4 target = prev } } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SOURCE$_band_5 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { add_to_variable_list = { name = $TARGET$_band_5 target = prev } } }
}

# Every final slot contains exactly one team in one of the five band lists.
# Copy that team's qualifying score to the host so teams from the same country
# can still be compared safely without temporary country-level state.
tot_music_competition_read_band_final_slot_score_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = $SCORE$ value = -1 }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_1 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { set_variable = { name = $SCORE$ value = prev.var:tot_music_competition_band_1_raw_score } } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_2 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { set_variable = { name = $SCORE$ value = prev.var:tot_music_competition_band_2_raw_score } } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_3 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { set_variable = { name = $SCORE$ value = prev.var:tot_music_competition_band_3_raw_score } } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_4 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { set_variable = { name = $SCORE$ value = prev.var:tot_music_competition_band_4_raw_score } } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_5 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no ROOT = { set_variable = { name = $SCORE$ value = prev.var:tot_music_competition_band_5_raw_score } } }
}

tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_match_effect = {
	tot_music_competition_read_band_final_slot_score_effect = { SLOT = $LEFT$ SCORE = tot_music_competition_final_left_score }
	tot_music_competition_read_band_final_slot_score_effect = { SLOT = $RIGHT$ SCORE = tot_music_competition_final_right_score }
	if = {
		limit = { var:tot_music_competition_final_left_score >= var:tot_music_competition_final_right_score }
		tot_music_competition_advance_band_final_slot_effect = { SOURCE = $LEFT$ TARGET = $TARGET$ }
	}
	else = {
		tot_music_competition_advance_band_final_slot_effect = { SOURCE = $RIGHT$ TARGET = $TARGET$ }
	}
	remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_left_score
	remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_right_score
}

tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_$BAND$_final_reward_paid } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_$BAND$_final_reward_paid value = 1 }
		tot_music_competition_apply_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = $BAND$ EXP = $EXP$ POINTS = $POINTS$ SKILL = $SKILL$ MODIFIER = $MODIFIER$ }
	}
}

tot_music_competition_apply_band_rewards_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_band_id value = $BAND$ }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain value = $EXP$ }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_reward_points value = $POINTS$ }
	# The legacy SKILL parameter now carries personal growth experience.
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_personal_exp_gain value = $SKILL$ }
	# Legacy calls used 250 / 1 / 1 for the round of sixteen. Keep those
	# repeated call sites compatible while promoting the actual reward tier.
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain = 250
			var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points = 1
			var:tot_band_pending_event_personal_exp_gain = 1
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain value = 1000 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_reward_points value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_personal_exp_gain value = 1000 }
	}
	tot_band_add_creation_experience_effect = yes
	tot_band_apply_reward_point_bonus_effect = yes
	tot_band_add_reward_points_effect = yes
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			is_character_alive = yes
			has_variable = tot_band_id
			var:tot_band_id = $BAND$
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_character_growth_exp } } set_variable = { name = tot_character_growth_exp value = 0 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_character_growth_exp add = root.var:tot_band_pending_event_personal_exp_gain }
		tot_character_growth_check_level_up = yes
	}
	remove_variable = tot_band_pending_event_personal_exp_gain
	add_modifier = { name = $MODIFIER$ days = 365 }
}

tot_music_competition_award_band_participation_effect = {
	every_country = {
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered } tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 1 EXP = 0 POINTS = 0 SKILL = 500 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_participant } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_2_registered } tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 2 EXP = 0 POINTS = 0 SKILL = 500 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_participant } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_3_registered } tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 3 EXP = 0 POINTS = 0 SKILL = 500 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_participant } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered } tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 4 EXP = 0 POINTS = 0 SKILL = 500 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_participant } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered } tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 5 EXP = 0 POINTS = 0 SKILL = 500 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_participant } }
	}
}

tot_music_competition_award_band_top_sixteen_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tot_music_competition_award_host
	set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_top_sixteen_count value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:tot_music_competition_award_host = { var:tot_music_competition_top_sixteen_count < 16 }
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_registered
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_score
			var:tot_music_competition_band_1_score = 54
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_1_top_sixteen_awarded }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_1_top_sixteen_awarded value = 1 }
		tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 1 EXP = 250 POINTS = 1 SKILL = 1 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_round_of_16 }

			var:tot_music_competition_band_2_score = 54

		tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 3 EXP = 250 POINTS = 1 SKILL = 1 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_round_of_16 }
		scope:tot_music_competition_award_host = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_top_sixteen_count add = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:tot_music_competition_award_host = { var:tot_music_competition_top_sixteen_count < 16 }
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_registered
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_score
			var:tot_music_competition_band_4_score = 0
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_4_top_sixteen_awarded }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_4_top_sixteen_awarded value = 1 }
		tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 4 EXP = 250 POINTS = 1 SKILL = 1 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_round_of_16 }
		scope:tot_music_competition_award_host = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_top_sixteen_count add = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:tot_music_competition_award_host = { var:tot_music_competition_top_sixteen_count < 16 }
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_registered
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_score
			var:tot_music_competition_band_5_score = 0
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_band_5_top_sixteen_awarded }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_band_5_top_sixteen_awarded value = 1 }
		tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 5 EXP = 250 POINTS = 1 SKILL = 1 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_round_of_16 }
		scope:tot_music_competition_award_host = { change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_top_sixteen_count add = 1 } }
	}
	remove_variable = tot_music_competition_top_sixteen_count
}

tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_effect = {
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_1 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no add_modifier = { name = $MODIFIER$ days = 365 } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_2 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no add_modifier = { name = $MODIFIER$ days = 365 } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_3 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no add_modifier = { name = $MODIFIER$ days = 365 } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_4 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no add_modifier = { name = $MODIFIER$ days = 365 } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_5 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no add_modifier = { name = $MODIFIER$ days = 365 } }
}

tot_music_competition_remove_band_final_slot_modifier_effect = {
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_1 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no remove_modifier = $MODIFIER$ }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_2 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no remove_modifier = $MODIFIER$ }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_3 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no remove_modifier = $MODIFIER$ }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_4 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no remove_modifier = $MODIFIER$ }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_5 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no remove_modifier = $MODIFIER$ }
}

tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = {
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_1 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 1 EXP = $EXP$ POINTS = $POINTS$ SKILL = $SKILL$ MODIFIER = $MODIFIER$ } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_2 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 2 EXP = $EXP$ POINTS = $POINTS$ SKILL = $SKILL$ MODIFIER = $MODIFIER$ } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_3 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 3 EXP = $EXP$ POINTS = $POINTS$ SKILL = $SKILL$ MODIFIER = $MODIFIER$ } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_4 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 4 EXP = $EXP$ POINTS = $POINTS$ SKILL = $SKILL$ MODIFIER = $MODIFIER$ } }
	ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_5 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 5 EXP = $EXP$ POINTS = $POINTS$ SKILL = $SKILL$ MODIFIER = $MODIFIER$ } }
}

# The finalist reward is given only to the losing finalist. The champion slot
# identifies the winning band even when both finalists are from one country.
tot_music_competition_award_band_runner_up_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_1 NOT = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_final_champion_band_1 } } ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_1 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 1 EXP = 4000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 4000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_finalist } } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_2 NOT = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_final_champion_band_2 } } ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_2 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 2 EXP = 4000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 4000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_finalist } } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_3 NOT = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_final_champion_band_3 } } ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_3 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 3 EXP = 4000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 4000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_finalist } } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_4 NOT = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_final_champion_band_4 } } ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_4 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 4 EXP = 4000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 4000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_finalist } } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = $SLOT$_band_5 NOT = { has_variable_list = tot_music_competition_final_champion_band_5 } } ordered_in_list = { variable = $SLOT$_band_5 position = 0 check_range_bounds = no tot_music_competition_award_band_rewards_effect = { BAND = 5 EXP = 4000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 4000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_finalist } } }
}

tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 has_variable = tot_music_competition_round var:tot_music_competition_round >= 8 NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_final_quarterfinal_resolved } }
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_match_effect = { LEFT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_01 RIGHT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_02 TARGET = tot_music_competition_final_four_01 }
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_match_effect = { LEFT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_03 RIGHT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_04 TARGET = tot_music_competition_final_four_02 }
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_match_effect = { LEFT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_05 RIGHT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_06 TARGET = tot_music_competition_final_four_03 }
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_match_effect = { LEFT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_07 RIGHT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_08 TARGET = tot_music_competition_final_four_04 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_quarterfinal_resolved value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 has_variable = tot_music_competition_round var:tot_music_competition_round >= 16 NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_final_semifinal_resolved } }
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_match_effect = { LEFT = tot_music_competition_final_four_01 RIGHT = tot_music_competition_final_four_02 TARGET = tot_music_competition_final_two_01 }
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_match_effect = { LEFT = tot_music_competition_final_four_03 RIGHT = tot_music_competition_final_four_04 TARGET = tot_music_competition_final_two_02 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_semifinal_resolved value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 has_variable = tot_music_competition_round var:tot_music_competition_round >= 26 NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_final_champion_resolved } }
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_match_effect = { LEFT = tot_music_competition_final_two_01 RIGHT = tot_music_competition_final_two_02 TARGET = tot_music_competition_final_champion }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_final_champion_resolved value = 1 }
	}
}

tot_music_competition_finish_final_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_music_competition_stage
			var:tot_music_competition_stage = 2
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_completed }
		}
		# The champion is resolved only after the full 26-week knockout schedule.
		tot_music_competition_resolve_band_final_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_track var:tot_music_competition_track = 1 }
				# Rewards are highest-rank-only. The guard inside the reward effect
				# prevents lower rounds from paying a second time.
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_champion EXP = 5000 POINTS = 3 SKILL = 5000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_champion }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_runner_up_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_two_01 }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_runner_up_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_two_02 }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_four_01 EXP = 3000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 3000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_semifinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_four_02 EXP = 3000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 3000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_semifinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_four_03 EXP = 3000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 3000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_semifinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_four_04 EXP = 3000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 3000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_semifinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_01 EXP = 2000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 2000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_quarterfinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_02 EXP = 2000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 2000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_quarterfinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_03 EXP = 2000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 2000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_quarterfinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_04 EXP = 2000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 2000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_quarterfinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_05 EXP = 2000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 2000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_quarterfinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_06 EXP = 2000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 2000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_quarterfinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_07 EXP = 2000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 2000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_quarterfinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_final_slot_rewards_effect = { SLOT = tot_music_competition_final_slot_08 EXP = 2000 POINTS = 2 SKILL = 2000 MODIFIER = modifier_tot_music_competition_quarterfinal }
				tot_music_competition_award_band_top_sixteen_effect = yes
				tot_music_competition_award_band_participation_effect = yes
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_completed value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_next_registration_days_remaining value = 730 }
		change_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_prize_money add = 1000 }
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_deadline
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_final_days_remaining
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_weekly_tick_scheduled
		remove_variable = tot_music_competition_next_tick
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_music_competition_award_announced } }
			set_variable = { name = tot_music_competition_award_announced value = 1 }
			trigger_event = { id = tot_music_competition.3 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = tot_music_competition.4 days = 730 }
	}
}
